10:["$","$L23",null,{"children":[["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"System:"}]," Unipi — all-in-one extension suite for the Pi coding agent (",["$","code",null,{"children":"@pi-unipi/unipi"}]," v2.18.x)\n",["$","strong",null,{"children":"Document type:"}]," C4 architecture model (Context → Container → Component → Code → Deployment)\n",["$","strong",null,{"children":"Generated:"}]," 2026-09-16 06:56:42 (UTC)"]}],"\n",["$","hr",null,{}],"\n",["$","$L24",null,{"as":"h2","id":"1-architecture-overview","children":"1. Architecture Overview"}],"\n",["$","$L24",null,{"as":"h3","id":"11-architecture-design-philosophy","children":"1.1 Architecture Design Philosophy"}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Unipi is not a standalone application. It is a ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"plugin suite"}]," that loads into an existing host — the Pi coding agent, a terminal-based AI coding assistant — and extends it with new tools, slash commands, lifecycle hooks, and terminal-UI overlays. Every architectural decision in the codebase follows from three constraints that this positioning imposes:"]}],"\n",["$","ol",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"The host owns the main loop."}]," No Unipi package runs its own event loop, HTTP listener (with the single exception of the kanboard board server and the trajectory server), or scheduler. Everything is triggered by the host: a tool call from the LLM, a slash command from the developer, or a lifecycle hook such as ",["$","code",null,{"children":"session_start"}],". Packages therefore behave as ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"reactive registrations"}]," rather than services."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"The agent's context window is the scarcest resource."}]," Compaction, bounded output truncation of helper results, token budgets for delegated children, and deterministic (non-LLM) summarization are all expressions of a single principle: never let a feature flood the parent conversation. This principle explains why ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@pi-unipi/core"}]," ships a ",["$","code",null,{"children":"bounded-output"}]," utility and why ",["$","code",null,{"children":"get_helper_result"}]," truncates before returning."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Optional peers must never become hard dependencies."}]," With ~24 independently publishable packages, any package may be absent or disabled. Cross-package cooperation is therefore designed around ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"discovery"}]," (",["$","code",null,{"children":"MODULE_READY"}]," events), ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"shared synchronous read-models"}]," (",["$","code",null,{"children":"Symbol.for"}]," global slots), and ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"file-format contracts"}]," (kanboard parses markdown written by workflow/milestone) instead of direct imports. The kernel ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@pi-unipi/core"}]," is the only universally shared code."]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["From these constraints emerge the operational values visible throughout the code: ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"config-gated, fail-closed registration"}]," (a disabled module registers nothing), ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"fail-soft backends"}]," (a missing MemPalace or a failed SQLite init degrades rather than crashes), ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"crash-safe persistence"}]," (temp-write/fsync/rename with versioned manifests), and ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"centralised process-tree lifecycle"}]," (timeouts, ESC abort propagation, and Windows ",["$","code",null,{"children":"taskkill"}]," tree termination are handled in hubs)."]}],"\n",["$","$L24",null,{"as":"h3","id":"12-core-architecture-patterns","children":"1.2 Core Architecture Patterns"}],"\n","$L25","\n","$L26","\n","$L27","\n","$L28","\n","$L29","\n","$L2a","\n","$L2b","\n","$L2c","\n","$L2d","\n","$L2e","\n","$L2f","\n","$L30","\n","$L31","\n","$L32","\n","$L33","\n","$L34","\n","$L35","\n","$L36","\n","$L37","\n","$L38","\n","$L39","\n","$L3a","\n","$L3b","\n","$L3c","\n","$L3d","\n","$L3e","\n","$L3f","\n","$L40","\n","$L41","\n","$L42","\n","$L43","\n","$L44","\n","$L45","\n","$L46","\n","$L47","\n","$L48","\n","$L49","\n","$L4a","\n","$L4b","\n","$L4c","\n","$L4d","\n","$L4e","\n","$L4f","\n","$L50","\n","$L51","\n","$L52","\n","$L53","\n","$L54","\n","$L55","\n","$L56","\n","$L57","\n","$L58","\n","$L59","\n","$L5a","\n","$L5b","\n","$L5c","\n","$L5d","\n","$L5e","\n","$L5f","\n","$L60","\n","$L61","\n","$L62","\n","$L63","\n","$L64","\n","$L65","\n","$L66","\n","$L67","\n","$L68","\n","$L69","\n","$L6a","\n","$L6b","\n","$L6c","\n","$L6d","\n","$L6e","\n","$L6f","\n","$L70","\n","$L71","\n","$L72","\n","$L73","\n","$L74","\n","$L75","\n","$L76","\n","$L77","\n","$L78","\n","$L79","\n","$L7a","\n","$L7b","\n","$L7c","\n","$L7d","\n","$L7e","\n","$L7f","\n","$L80","\n","$L81","\n","$L82","\n","$L83","\n","$L84","\n","$L85","\n","$L86","\n","$L87","\n","$L88","\n","$L89","\n","$L8a","\n","$L8b","\n","$L8c","\n","$L8d","\n","$L8e","\n","$L8f","\n","$L90","\n","$L91","\n","$L92","\n","$L93","\n","$L94","\n","$L95","\n","$L96","\n","$L97","\n","$L98","\n","$L99","\n","$L9a","\n","$L9b","\n","$L9c","\n","$L9d","\n","$L9e","\n","$L9f","\n","$La0","\n","$La1","\n","$La2","\n","$La3","\n","$La4","\n","$La5","\n","$La6","\n","$La7","\n","$La8","\n","$La9","\n","$Laa","\n","$Lab","\n","$Lac","\n","$Lad","\n","$Lae","\n","$Laf","\n","$Lb0","\n","$Lb1","\n","$Lb2","\n","$Lb3","\n","$Lb4","\n","$Lb5","\n","$Lb6","\n","$Lb7","\n","$Lb8","\n","$Lb9","\n","$Lba"]}]

2b:["$","p",null,{"children":"Unipi turns a single-process terminal coding agent into a broader development platform. Its business value can be summarised in four capabilities that map directly onto user needs:"}]
2c:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Longer autonomous sessions"}]," — the compactor keeps context under budget with a deterministic summarisation pipeline and persists session history to SQLite for later recall."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Parallel and long-running execution"}]," — subagents and background tasks let the primary agent delegate to child Pi processes with budgets, artifacts, and result packages."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Persistent memory"}]," — the memory package stores durable knowledge in MemPalace with a human-readable markdown tier."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"External reach"}]," — MCP servers, web search/fetch, image generation/recognition, and model fusion extend what the agent can call."]}],"\n"]}]
2d:["$","p",null,{"children":"Around these core capabilities sit presentation features (footer status bar, ask-user prompts, keyboard chords, notifications) and team-process features (workflow commands with sandboxes, milestones, a kanban web UI)."}]

5e:["$","p",null,{"children":["The coupling in this domain is ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"file-format based"}],".
